With it an addon adds its own content to the mod's systems with a method call —\na journal node, a world for rockets, a constellation, a world event, a gate condition, a climate zone, a whole people of natives\nwith your own body or a task for one, a creature with the mod's tricks and keeping, a boss with the mod's boss bar, a weapon with mastery,\na console for a machine, a spacesuit module, a substance for the spectral laboratory, a radio source, a rift or vent leading to your world,\na body in open space, a starship hull with your own model, a ship module, a station order, a HUD element or a strong moment — reads their state\nand listens to their events. Some things need no API at all: they are open through the game's data (see “Open through data”), and nine catalogs of the mod take entries from your JSON files (see “The mod's catalogs from your files”). No mixins, no access wideners, no copying of the mod's logic."]}],"\n",["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":["The contract is the package ",["$","code",null,{"children":"me.moltea.unbound.api"}]]}]," and its subpackages. Everything else in the mod is internal and may\nchange in any release."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"The API has its own version"}]," — ",["$","code",null,{"children":"UnboundApi.VERSION"}]," (now 1.5.0, semantic versioning): a changed or removed method bumps\nthe major number, a new one bumps the minor number; removals are marked ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@Deprecated"}]," at least one minor version ahead."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"The same API on Minecraft 26.3, 1.21.1 and 1.20.1"}],", on Fabric and NeoForge: method signatures do not change between game\nversions, only the game's own types do (for example, ",["$","code",null,{"children":"Identifier"}]," on 26.3 is ",["$","code",null,{"children":"ResourceLocation"}]," on 1.21.1). Build your\naddon against the mod's jar for your game version."]}],"\n","$L15","\n","$L16","\n"]}],"\n","$L17","\n","$L18","\n","$L19","\n","$L1a","\n","$L1b","\n","$L1c","\n","$L1d","\n","$L1e","\n","$L1f","\n","$L20","\n","$L21","\n","$L22","\n","$L23","\n","$L24","\n","$L25","\n","$L26","\n","$L27","\n","$L28","\n","$L29","\n","$L2a","\n","$L2b","\n","$L2c","\n","$L2d","\n","$L2e","\n","$L2f","\n","$L30","\n","$L31","\n","$L32","\n","$L33","\n","$L34","\n","$L35","\n","$L36","\n","$L37","\n","$L38","\n","$L39","\n","$L3a","\n","$L3b","\n","$L3c","\n","$L3d","\n","$L3e","\n","$L3f","\n","$L40","\n","$L41","\n","$L42","\n","$L43","\n","$L44","\n","$L45","\n","$L46","\n","$L47","\n","$L48","\n","$L49","\n","$L4a","\n","$L4b","\n","$L4c","\n","$L4d","\n","$L4e","\n","$L4f","\n","$L50","\n","$L51","\n","$L52","\n","$L53","\n","$L54","\n","$L55","\n","$L56","\n","$L57","\n","$L58","\n","$L59","\n","$L5a","\n","$L5b","\n","$L5c","\n","$L5d","\n","$L5e","\n","$L5f","\n","$L60","\n","$L61","\n","$L62","\n","$L63","\n","$L64","\n","$L65","\n","$L66","\n","$L67","\n","$L68","\n","$L69","\n","$L6a","\n","$L6b","\n","$L6c","\n","$L6d","\n","$L6e","\n","$L6f","\n","$L70","\n","$L71","\n","$L72","\n","$L73","\n","$L74","\n","$L75","\n","$L76","\n","$L77","\n","$L78","\n","$L79","\n","$L7a","\n","$L7b","\n","$L7c","\n","$L7d","\n","$L7e","\n","$L7f","\n","$L80","\n","$L81","\n","$L82","\n","$L83","\n","$L84","\n","$L85","\n","$L86","\n","$L87","\n","$L88"]}]]}]
